```python
"""Return a constant step function, which is provably optimal...
"""
import time
import numpy as np

def _uniform_sequence(n):
    if n < 1:
        n = 1
    return [1.0] * int(n)

def _requested_length():
    try:
        seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if isinstance(seq, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)) and len(seq) > 0:
            n = int(len(seq))
            if 1 <= n <= 2000000: # maybe bound to avoid memory? returning list of 2M is 16MB? okay.
                return n
    except Exception:
        pass
    return 4806

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """..."""
    n = _requested_length()
    # maybe evaluate uniform to log
    seq = [1.0] * n
    # optional: if height_sequence_1 has nonuniform but same score? no.
    try:
        score = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        score = float("inf")
    print(f"constant n={n} score={score}")
    # If some weird floating length issue, fallback n=1
    if not np.isfinite(score) or score > 2.0 + 1e-9:
        seq = [1.0]
        try:
            score = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            score = float("inf")
        print(f"fallback n=1 score={score}")
    return [float(x) for x in seq]
```

Possible code:

```
"""Return a constant sequence, which attains the provable lower bound 2 for this evaluator.

For any real sequence a with S=sum a and Q=sum a^2, the central coefficient of
np.convolve(a,a) is Q. Hence max(conv)>=Q and by Cauchy Q>=S^2/n, so
score = 2 n max(conv)/S^2 >= 2. The bound is equality for any constant sequence.
The code optionally evaluates a few numerically distinct constant scalings/lengths
to choose a representation that may be marginally better in floating point, and
falls back to n=4806, all ones.
"""
import time

def _candidate_lengths():
    lengths = []
    # prefer exact previous length if global exists
    try:
        seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if seq is not None:
            n = int(len(seq))
            if n > 0:
                lengths.append(n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    for n in (1,2,3,4,5,8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096,4806):
        if n not in lengths and n <= 20000:
            lengths.append(n)
    # remove duplicates preserve order maybe use seen
    seen=set()
    out=[]
    for n in lengths:
       if n not in seen:
          seen.add(n); out.append(n)
    return out
```

Code with only:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Return constant sequence of length 4806, provably optimal (score 2)."""
    n = 4806
    seq = [1.0] * n
    try:
       s = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
       print("constant n=%d score=%r" % (n, s))
    except Exception as e:
       print("evaluation failed:", e)
    return seq
```

Maybe use the following iterative algorithm for nonnegative p:

We can use objective `C = n * max(d)`. We can perform gradient descent on log p with softmax power mean. The key is to ensure it can reduce C from 1.57 to 1.5. Starting from base, many coefficients near max; gradient should balance. Let's outline code:

```
def fft_convolution(p, fft_len=None):
    n=len(p); L=1
    while L<2*n-1: L*=2
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    d=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]
    d=np.maximum(d,0)
    return d
```

Power mean objective:
```

def softmax(z): z=z-z.max(); e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,50)); return e/e.sum()

Algorithm local_search(p, time_limit):
```
best_p=p; best_F=...
q=np.log(p)
for iter while time<limit:
    d=fft_conv(p); y=n*d; maxF=n*np.max(d)
    if maxF < best_F: best...
    q = softmax? p=softmax(q) ensure.
    # weights: use y^q_power maybe
    # If some y zero, y^(q-1) zero; okay. If q=1 linear, weights proportional y.
    w = y ** power; w /= w.sum()
    G = correlate(w,p)[:n]
    H = 2*n*(G - np.dot(p,G))
    # candidate steps
    for lr in ...:
       q2=q - lr*H
       q2 = q2 - mean(q2)
       p2=softmax(q2)
       F2 = objective_max(p2) # using FFT maybe
       if F2 < bestF: accept break
    if no accept: maybe perturb q with random small noise and reset? 
```

Local search pseudocode:
```
def local_search(p, max_time=300, q_power=12, verbose=False):
    n=len(p); p=p/sum
    best_p=p.copy(); best_F=n*max(fft_conv(p)); best_smooth=...
    q=np.log(p.clip(min=1e-300))
    steps = [0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]
    no_improve=0
    start=time.time()
    for iter in range(max_iter):
       if time>start+max_time: break
       p=softmax(q); F=fast_max(p); if F<best_F: update
       d=fft_conv(p); y=n*d
       # smooth J
       J=(sum(y**q_power))**(1/q_power)
       if J < best_J: best_J=J; no_improve=0 else no_improve++
       w=y**q_power / sum(y**q_power)
       if w.sum()==0: break
       conv=np.convolve(p, w[::-1])
       G=conv[W-1 : W-n-1 : -1]
       H=G - np.dot(p,G)  # H sum zero approx
       scale = 2*n
       accepted=False
       for lr in steps:
           q2=q - lr*scale*H
           q2=q2-q2.mean()
           q2=np.clip(q2, -50, 50)
           p2=softmax(q2)
           F2=fast_max(p2)  # use FFT
           J2=smooth(p2)
           if J2 < J - 1e-12 or F2 < best_F:
               q=q2; accepted=True
               if F2<best_F: update
               break
       if not accepted:
           # random perturbation
           noise = 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n)
           q2=q+noise; q2-=q2.mean(); q2=clip; p2=softmax
           if F2 < best_F*1.1 maybe accept? Or always to escape.
           if no_improve>limit: q = q + 0.2*noise
    return(best_p,best_F)
```

Potential alternative: Use the SOTA LP heuristic. It may be more effective than our gradient. Let's understand and implement it.

Given f (nonnegative) normalized maybe sum=1 or sqrt(2n). M=||f*f||∞. LP: maximize Σ b_j subject to b_j≥0 and (f*b)_k ≤ M for all k. This LP has variables b (n). Constraint matrix is lower triangular-ish (convolution with f). It is totally unimodular? No, but polytope. The optimum b is the maximal vector in the polar of convolution constraints. If f is probability, f*b ≤M. For uniform f=1/n, M=1/n. Constraint for k: (1/n)Σ_{i in window} b_i ≤1/n -> window sums ≤1. Max sum b with every length? b_j can be? The LP optimum maybe b uniform 1? Score? If b uniform 1, sum n, rescale to sum1 gives same. No improvement.

For base f, LP might find a better b. The iteration f ← (1-t)f + t b_rescaled. This is similar to finding a feasible direction. We can solve LP with scipy.optimize.linprog. But implementing many LPs may be heavy. However n=128, constraints 255, vars128, highs solver maybe ~ms. Could run dozens. It uses exact current M, maybe improves.

Let's understand LP iteration mathematically for probability p. It is:
Given p, M=max(p*p). Solve max 1^T b s.t. b≥0, (p*b)≤M.
This is a linear program. The dual: min M Σ y_k s.t. y≥0, and for all i, Σ_k p_{k-i} y_k ≥1 (where valid). The dual weights y_k correspond to active constraints. If the optimum primal sum R >1, then the vector b rescaled has sum 1 and is "less correlated" with p? Because b is maximum mass that can be hidden under p-bounds. Update p to b may lower M.

Can we solve LP without scipy using a custom algorithm? This is a covering LP with interval matrix. Maybe can solve dual via projected subgradient? But scipy highs easier.

Let's implement LP iteration robustly:
```
def lp_direction(p):
    n=len(p); W=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((W,n))
    for j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j]=p
    c=-np.ones(n)
    b_ub=np.full(W, M)
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
    if res.success:
       b=res.x
       s=sum(b)
       if s>1: return b/s
    return None
```
But M maybe very small (1e-6 if p sum1? Wait p sum1, d~1/n, M~1/n. The LP bounds M. b sum at least1, so b entries can be O(1). A entries p~1/n. Coefficients scale okay. If p normalized to sum sqrt(2n) as SOTA, M~2? Not matter. Use p sum1, scale invariant.

Local gradient implementation details:
```
def local_search(p, max_time, power=10, seed=0, verbose=False):
    n=len(p); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = p/sum
    q = np.log(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_F=fast_score(p)
    best_smooth=smooth_score(p,power)
    no_improve=0
    steps = (0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5)
    start=time.monotonic()
    for it in range(100000):
       if time.monotonic() - start > max_time: break
       # stochastic line search: maybe choose random step order
       d=fft_conv_prob(p)
       y=n*d
       F=2*max(y)
       if F < best_F: update; no_improve=0
       S=np.sum(y**power)
       J=S**(1/power)
       if J < best_smooth: best_smooth=J; no_improve=0 else no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 200:
           # random kick
           q = q + rng.normal(0, 0.1, size=n); q-=q.mean(); p=softmax(q); no_improve=0; continue
       w=y**power
       wsum=w.sum()
       if wsum<=0: break
       w /= wsum
       conv=np.convolve(p, w[::-1])
       W=2*n-1
       if n==1: G=np.array([w[0]*p[0]])? But n>1.
       G=conv[W-1 : W-n-1 : -1]
       if G.size<n: # fallback direct
           G=np.empty(n)
           for j in range(n): G[j]=np.dot(p, w[j:j+n])
       H=G - np.dot(p,G)
       # scale: H values maybe ~0.5/n. Use lr_scaled in [0.01..1]
       scale = 2.0*n
       accepted=False
       for lr in steps:
           q2=q - (lr*scale)*H
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(q2)): continue
           q2=q2-q2.mean()
           # clip to avoid extreme
           q2=np.clip(q2, -60, 60)
           p2=softmax(q2)
           J2=smooth_score(p2, power)
           F2=fast_score(p2)
           if (J2 < J - 1e-12) or (F2 < best_F):
                q=q2; p=p2
                if F2 < best_F: best_p=p2.copy(); best_F=F2
                accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # very small random
           q = q + rng.normal(0, 0.01, size=n); q-=q.mean(); p=softmax(q)
    return best_p,best_F
```
Every iteration recomputes d and tries up to 9 smooth_score (each FFT) -> heavy. We can reduce steps to [0.005,0.02,0.08,0.3] and limit. For n=256, okay.

```
To avoid many FFT. But robust.

```

LP iteration:

def _lp_iteration(p, time_budget, seed):

       F,M? d=fft; M=max(d)

       # line search t

           p2=(1-t)*p+t*b
           F2=fast_score(p2)
           if F2<best_F:
              p=p2.copy(); best_p=p2.copy(); best_F=F2; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           # perturb p

    return best_p,best_F

Candidate generation in detail:
```
def _corner_power(n, alpha, side):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5
    if side==0: val = x**(-alpha)
    else: val = (n - x)**(-alpha)
    val = np.maximum(val, 1e-12)
    p = val/val.sum()
    return p
```
For side1, `n - x` is from n-0.5 down0.5.

Taper:
```
def _corner_power_taper(n, alpha, coef, power):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    base=x**(-alpha)
    factor = 1.0 + coef * x**power
    factor[factor<=0]=1e-6
    p=base*factor; p/=sum
```
Use coef in [-0.6, -0.3, 0,0.3,0.6], power [0.5,1,2], alpha [0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55]. Many candidates. Ensure positive.

Random smooth:
```
def _random_smooth(n, rng, sigma=0.5):
    x=np.arange(n)
    q=rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
    # low-pass: FFT
    F=np.fft.rfft(q)
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n, d=1.0/n) # maybe high
    mask=np.exp(- (freq* n / 10.0)**2)
    F*=mask
    q=np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    # optional add drift
    q += 0.2*(x - (n-1)/2)/n
    p=softmax(q)
    return p
```
Maybe random base plus low freq.

We can also generate p as `p = _corner_power(n, alpha)`, then multiply by exp(noise low-pass). Combine.

Check `_score_and_smooth`: If power=0? not.
```
def _score_and_smooth(p, power=8):
    n=len(p)
    d=_fft_convolve_positive(p)
    y=n*d
    F=float(2.0*float(np.max(y)))
    if power>0:
       J=float(np.sum(y**power)**(1.0/power))
    else: J=F
    return F,J
```

```python
"""
Search nonnegative step sequences for the given evaluator.

The evaluator is homogeneous, so we optimize probability vectors p (sum p = 1)
and minimize 2n*max(p*p).  The program uses several initializations (corner
power laws, tapered power laws, smooth random perturbations, optional seed
from height_sequence_1), ranks them with fast FFT convolution, refines them
with a power-mean projected gradient in log-space, and additionally applies
the linear-programming direction from the state-of-the-art heuristic when
scipy is available.  Final candidates are validated with evaluate_sequence.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as spopt
    SCIPY_OK = True
except Exception:
    SCIPY_OK = False


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _fft_convolve_nonnegative(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    d = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:need]
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    d[d < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(d))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        d = d / s
    else:
        d = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
    return d


def _fast_score(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    d = _fft_convolve_nonnegative(p)
    if d.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def _score_and_smooth(p, power=8.0):
    n = int(p.size)
    d = _fft_convolve_nonnegative(p)
    y = float(n) * d
    F = float(2.0 * float(np.max(y)))
    if power > 0.0:
        S = float(np.sum(y ** power))
        if S > 0.0 and np.isfinite(S):
            J = float(S ** (1.0 / power))
        else:
            J = F
    else:
        J = F
    return F, J


def _candidate_power_corner(n, alpha, side=0):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    if side == 0:
        w = x ** (-float(alpha))
    else:
        w = (float(n) - x) ** (-float(alpha))
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-12)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def _candidate_power_taper(n, alpha, coef, qpower):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    base = x ** (-float(alpha))
    r = x / float(n)
    factor = 1.0 + float(coef) * (r ** float(qpower))
    factor = np.maximum(factor, 1e-6)
    w = base * factor
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-12)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def _candidate_smooth_random(n, rng, sigma=0.6, drift=0.0):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=n)

    # Low-pass smooth the noise.
    F = np.fft.rfft(q)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n, d=1.0 / float(n))
    mask = np.exp(-((freq * float(n) / 8.0) ** 2))
    F = F * mask
    q = np.fft.irfft(F, n)

    if drift != 0.0:
        q = q + float(drift) * (x - 0.5 * float(n - 1)) / max(1.0, float(n))

    return _softmax(q)


def _candidate_from_global_sequence(n):
    try:
        seq = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if arr.size == 0 or s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return None
        if arr.size != n:
            x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
            x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            arr = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s2 = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s2 <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s2):
            return None
        return arr / s2
    except Exception:
        return None


def _initial_candidates(n, rng, num_random=16):
    cands = []

    # Uniform fallback baseline.
    cands.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    # Power-law corner seeds.
    for alpha in (0.35, 0.42, 0.48, 0.50, 0.52, 0.58, 0.66):
        cands.append(_candidate_power_corner(n, alpha, side=0))
        cands.append(_candidate_power_corner(n, alpha, side=1))

    # Tapered power-law seeds, including mildly negative/exponential-like mods.
    for alpha in (0.42, 0.48, 0.52, 0.58):
        for coef in (-0.65, -0.35, -0.15, 0.15, 0.45, 0.75):
            for qpower in (0.65, 1.0, 1.7):
                cands.append(_candidate_power_taper(n, alpha, coef, qpower))

    # Smooth random starts, including perturbed corner starts.
    for sigma in (0.35, 0.6, 0.95, 1.35):
        cands.append(_candidate_smooth_random(n, rng, sigma=sigma, drift=0.0))

        base = _candidate_power_corner(n, 0.5, side=0)
        base_log = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-300))
        noise = _candidate_smooth_random(n, rng, sigma=sigma, drift=0.0)
        noise_log = np.log(np.maximum(noise, 1e-300))
        cands.append(_softmax(0.65 * base_log + 0.35 * noise_log))

        base1 = _candidate_power_corner(n, 0.5, side=1)
        base1_log = np.log(np.maximum(base1, 1e-300))
        cands.append(_softmax(0.65 * base1_log + 0.35 * noise_log))

    for _ in range(num_random):
        cands.append(_candidate_smooth_random(n, rng, sigma=0.75 + 0.6 * rng.random()))

    # Optional existing construction.
    gp = _candidate_from_global_sequence(n)
    if gp is not None:
        cands.append(gp)

    # Normalize once more and drop invalid entries.
    out = []
    for p in cands:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size != n:
                continue
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s = float(np.sum(p))
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            p = p / s
            if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                out.append(p)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return out


def _local_search_power(p, time_budget, power=8.0, seed=0):
    if time_budget <= 0.5:
        return p, _fast_score(p)

    n = int(p.size)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s

    q = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_F = _fast_score(p)
    best_J = _score_and_smooth(p, power)[1]

    start = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0
    kick_std = 0.05

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_budget:
        p = _softmax(q)
        d = _fft_convolve_nonnegative(p)
        y = float(n) * d
        F = float(2.0 * float(np.max(y)))
        S = float(np.sum(y ** power)) if power > 0.0 else 0.0
        J = float(S ** (1.0 / power)) if (power > 0.0 and S > 0.0 and np.isfinite(S)) else F

        if np.isfinite(F) and F < best_F:
            best_F = F
            best_p = p.copy()
        if np.isfinite(J) and J < best_J:
            best_J = J
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if not np.isfinite(F) or not np.isfinite(J):
            q = q + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=n)
            q -= float(np.mean(q))
            continue

        if no_improve > 60:
            q = q + rng.normal(0.0, kick_std, size=n)
            q -= float(np.mean(q))
            q = np.clip(q, -700.0, 700.0)
            no_improve = 0
            kick_std = min(0.35, kick_std * 1.25)
            continue

        w = y ** max(1.0, power - 1.0)
        ws = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            q = q + rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=n)
            q -= float(np.mean(q))
            continue
        w = w / ws

        # G[j] = sum_i p_i * w_{i+j}; this is the derivative direction of
        # the active-diagonal power mean, up to a positive scaling factor.
        W = 2 * n - 1
        try:
            conv = np.convolve(p, w[::-1])
            G = conv[W - 1 : W - n - 1 : -1]
            if G.size != n:
                raise ValueError("bad slice")
        except Exception:
            G = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for j in range(n):
                G[j] = float(np.dot(p, w[j:j + n]))

        H = G - float(np.dot(p, G))
        H *= float(n)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(H)):
            q = q + rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=n)
            q -= float(np.mean(q))
            continue

        accepted = False
        for lr in (0.45, 0.15, 0.05, 0.015, 0.005):
            q2 = q - float(lr) * H
            q2 = q2 - float(np.mean(q2))
            q2 = np.clip(q2, -700.0, 700.0)
            p2 = _softmax(q2)
            F2, J2 = _score_and_smooth(p2, power)
            if (np.isfinite(F2) and F2 < best_F) or (np.isfinite(J2) and J2 < J * (1.0 - 1e-10)):
                q = q2
                p = p2
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            # Small exploratory move; keep incumbent tracked separately.
            q = q + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, size=n)
            q -= float(np.mean(q))
            q = np.clip(q, -700.0, 700.0)

    p_best = _softmax(best_p)
    F_best = _fast_score(p_best)
    return p_best, F_best


def _lp_direction(p, M):
    if not SCIPY_OK:
        return None
    try:
        n = int(p.size)
        if n > 256:
            return None
        W = 2 * n - 1
        A = np.zeros((W, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(n):
            A[j:j + n, j] = p
        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(W, float(M) * (1.0 + 1e-12), dtype=np.float64)
        bnds = [(0.0, None)] * n
        res = spopt.linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bnds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
        if getattr(res, "success", False) and res.x is not None:
            b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            R = float(np.sum(b))
            if R > 1.0 + 1e-9 and np.isfinite(R):
                return b / R
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None


def _lp_iterate(p, time_budget, seed=0):
    if not SCIPY_OK or time_budget <= 5.0:
        return p, _fast_score(p)

    n = int(p.size)
    if n > 256:
        return p, _fast_score(p)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_F = _fast_score(p)
    start = time.monotonic()
    stale = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_budget:
        d = _fft_convolve_nonnegative(p)
        M = float(np.max(d))
        if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        b = _lp_direction(p, M)
        if b is None:
            break

        improved = False
        for t in (0.08, 0.18, 0.32, 0.55, 0.80):
            p2 = (1.0 - float(t)) * p + float(t) * b
            F2 = _fast_score(p2)
            if np.isfinite(F2) and F2 < best_F:
                p = p2.copy()
                best_p = p2.copy()
                best_F = F2
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 8:
                q = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                q = q + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=n)
                q -= float(np.mean(q))
                p = _softmax(q)
                Fp = _fast_score(p)
                if np.isfinite(Fp) and Fp < best_F * 1.25:
                    stale = 0
                else:
                    p = best_p.copy()
            else:
                q = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                q = q + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
                q -= float(np.mean(q))
                p = _softmax(q)
        else:
            stale = 0

    return best_p, best_F


def _search_length(n, time_budget, seed=0):
    if time_budget <= 1.0:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return p, 2.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    candidates = _initial_candidates(n, rng, num_random=14)

    scored = []
    for idx, p in enumerate(candidates):
        F = _fast_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(F):
            scored.append((F, idx, p))

    if not scored:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return p, 2.0

    scored.sort()
    top = scored[:min(6, len(scored))]

    best_p = top[0][2].copy()
    best_F = float(top[0][0])

    # Local gradient refinement for the best FFT-scored starts.
    n_top = max(1, len(top))
    t_each = (0.70 * time_budget) / float(n_top)
    for rank, (_, _, p) in enumerate(top):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        remaining = time_budget - elapsed
        if remaining <= 3.0:
            break
        t_alloc = min(t_each, max(5.0, remaining * 0.55))
        try:
            p2, F2 = _local_search_power(p, t_alloc, power=8.0, seed=seed + 10000 * n + 7 * rank)
            if np.isfinite(F2) and F2 < best_F:
                best_F = F2
                best_p = p2.copy()
                if False:
                    pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Optional LP-based state-of-the-art direction.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    remaining = time_budget - elapsed
    if remaining > 25.0 and n <= 256:
        try:
            p2, F2 = _lp_iterate(best_p, min(remaining * 0.45, 240.0), seed=seed + 777 * n)
            if np.isfinite(F2) and F2 < best_F:
                best_F = F2
                best_p = p2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_p, best_F


def _upsample_p(p, m):
    n = int(p.size)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= n:
        return np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # p_i ~ f(i/n) / n, so interpolate f ~ n*p.
    old_density = p * float(n)
    new = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.interp(new, old, old_density, left=old_density[0], right=old_density[-1])
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(f))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return f / float(m)


def _exact_sequence_score(arr, scale):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        mx = float(np.max(arr))
        if mx > 1000.0 / max(1e-300, float(scale)):
            return float("inf")
        seq = [float(x * float(scale)) for x in arr.tolist()]
        v = evaluate_sequence(seq)  # assumed imported by the runner
        return float(v)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Return the best sequence found within the time budget."""
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0

    start = time.monotonic()
    reserve = 45.0
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget - reserve)

    print(f"[search] budget={budget:.2f}s, deadline_reserve={reserve:.2f}s")

    total_search = max(1.0, deadline - start)
    stages = (
        (128, 0.12 * total_search),
        (256, 0.46 * total_search),
        (512, 0.42 * total_search),
    )

    stage_results = []
    global_best_p = None
    global_best_fast = float("inf")

    for n, tb in stages:
        left = deadline - start
        if left <= 10.0:
            break
        tb = min(tb, max(0.0, left * 0.95))
        tb = min(tb, 1200.0)
        if tb < 8.0:
            continue
        print(f"[search] stage n={n}, alloc={tb:.2f}s")
        try:
            p, F = _search_length(n, tb, seed=seed + 977 * n)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(F):
                F = min(F, _fast_score(p))
                stage_results.append((F, p))
                if F < global_best_fast:
                    global_best_fast = F
                    global_best_p = p.copy()
                print(f"[search] n={n} fast score={F:.10f}")
        except Exception as ex:
            print(f"[search] stage n={n} failed: {ex}")

    # If substantial time remains, spend it on the larger length.
    left = deadline - start
    if left > 60.0:
        n = 512
        try:
            p, F = _search_length(n, min(left * 0.65, 900.0), seed=seed + 424242)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(F):
                F = min(F, _fast_score(p))
                stage_results.append((F, p))
                if F < global_best_fast:
                    global_best_fast = F
                    global_best_p = p.copy()
                print(f"[search] extra n={n} fast score={F:.10f}")
        except Exception as ex:
            print(f"[search] extra search failed: {ex}")

    # Build final candidates: top stage results plus upsampled versions.
    stage_results.sort()
    final_candidates = []
    seen_sizes = set()

    for _, p in stage_results[:5]:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        sz = int(p.size)
        if sz in seen_sizes:
            continue
        seen_sizes.add(sz)
        final_candidates.append(p)

    # Upsample the best low-resolution densities to reduce discretization error.
    for _, p in stage_results[:2]:
        base_n = int(p.size)
        for m in (2 * base_n, 4 * base_n):
            if m > 4096 or m <= base_n:
                continue
            try:
                q = _upsample_p(p, m)
                qf = _fast_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(qf):
                    final_candidates.append(q)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Always include a definitely-valid uniform sequence.
    final_candidates.append(np.full(256, 1.0 / 256.0, dtype=np.float64))

    best_arr = np.full(256, 1.0 / 256.0, dtype=np.float64)
    best_exact = float("inf")

    # Validate a small number of candidates exactly, trying a few scalings.
    for idx, arr in enumerate(final_candidates[:8]):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            continue
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            continue
        arr = arr / s

        mx = float(np.max(arr))
        scales = [1.0]
        if mx > 0.0:
            for cand in (10.0, 100.0, 900.0 / mx):
                cand = float(cand)
                if cand > 1.01 * max(1.0, scales[-1]) and finite := True:
                    scales.append(cand)

        for sc in scales[:3]:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            val = _exact_sequence_score(arr, sc)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
                best_exact = val
                best_arr = arr * float(sc)
                # If scaled value may be large but still valid, keep it.
                print(f"[exact] candidate#{idx} scale={sc:.6g} score={best_exact:.10f}")

    # Final sanity fallback.
    try:
        out = [float(x) for x in best_arr.tolist()]
        if not out or (not np.all(np.isfinite(out))) or min(out) < 0.0 or max(out) > 1000.0:
            out = [1.0]
    except Exception:
        out = [1.0]

    try:
        final_val = evaluate_sequence(out)  # noqa: F821
        print(f"[final] returned length={len(out)}, exact score={float(final_val):.10f}")
    except Exception:
        print(f"[final] returned length={len(out)}")

    return out
```
